# NFL Prop Engine Feature Dictionary

## 1. Scope and authority

This dictionary is generated from the canonical local feature contracts:

- `data/historical/features/feature_manifest.json`
- `config/features/*.json`

The canonical player-game grain is `season + week + game_id + player_id`.

The machine-readable manifests remain authoritative for exact feature membership. This document records human-readable definition, source, units, chronology/as-of policy, nullable behavior, and current model use.

A row classified as a training/evaluation label or audit/provenance column is documented for completeness but is **not** a production model predictor.

Missing feature values are not globally converted to zero. A zero is valid only when the feature's builder explicitly defines zero as the correct semantic value.

All predictor features must obey the pregame/as-of rules in `docs/DATA_CONTRACT.md`.

## 2. Temporal naming contract

| Suffix | Definition |
|---|---|
| `_lag1` | Most recent strictly prior source-game value. |
| `_rollN_mean` | Arithmetic mean over up to the last N strictly prior source games. |
| `_rollN_median` | Median over up to the last N strictly prior source games. |
| `_rollN_std` | Population standard deviation over up to the last N strictly prior source games. |
| `_ewmN` | Exponentially weighted mean over strictly prior source games with span N and `adjust=False`. |
| `_season_to_date` | Arithmetic mean over available earlier games in the current target season. |
| `_career_prior` | Arithmetic mean over available strictly prior career history; team-share metrics reset by current-franchise stint. |
| `_season_prior` | Specialized prior-season/current-season history available before the target game, as emitted by its builder. |

Player-history semantics above are implemented by `scripts/build/build_player_form.py`; no target-game observation is included in these states.

## 3. Source-family map

| Family | Primary source |
|---|---|
| `audit` | Audit metadata emitted by `scripts/build/build_historical_features.py`; used to prove chronology and source provenance, not as a model predictor. |
| `context` | Historical/current player-game universe and schedule context; `data/historical/universe/player_game_universe.parquet` for historical rows. |
| `environment` | `data/historical/features/environment.parquet`; historical schedule/weather/travel context from `scripts/build/build_environment_history.py`; current target-game weather/travel is overlaid by `scripts/project/build_current_features.py`. |
| `history` | Strict-prior player-history availability/stint state derived from player form/universe during final feature assembly. |
| `matchup` | Derived during `scripts/build/build_historical_features.py` from strict-prior player/team/opponent history and `data/historical/opportunity/position_allowed_week.parquet`; recomputed for current weeks by `scripts/project/build_current_features.py`. |
| `opponent` | `data/historical/features/opponent_form.parquet`; strict-prior opponent/defensive history built by `scripts/build/build_team_form.py`. |
| `player` | `data/historical/features/player_form.parquet`; strict-prior player history built by `scripts/build/build_player_form.py`, with canonical player opportunity/stat inputs. |
| `role` | `data/historical/features/player_role_history.parquet`; built by `scripts/build/build_role_history.py`. |
| `target` | `data/historical/targets/player_game_targets.parquet`; realized evaluation label from `scripts/build/build_targets.py`. Never a production model predictor. |
| `team` | `data/historical/features/team_form.parquet`; strict-prior team history built by `scripts/build/build_team_form.py`. |

- Historical-manifest features/columns covered: **798**
- Target-config predictor features covered: **494**
- Unique dictionary records: **798**
- Target feature-config files: **9**

- `audit`: 7
- `context`: 13
- `environment`: 21
- `history`: 3
- `matchup`: 26
- `opponent`: 126
- `player`: 422
- `role`: 31
- `target`: 9
- `team`: 140

## 7. Maintenance rule

Whenever `feature_manifest.json` or any `config/features/*.json` file changes, regenerate this dictionary with the Item 50 generator. The generator must fail rather than silently omit a new feature.

The following are hard failures:

- a configured predictor absent from the dictionary;
- a manifest feature/column absent from the dictionary;
- a dictionary record with blank definition, source, units, as-of rule, or nullable behavior;
- a target feature config referencing an undeclared required model feature;
- duplicate dictionary feature records.
